H-L\n\ndef excel_percentile_inc(data, p):\n    # data: list of floats, p in [0,1]\n    xs = sorted(float(x) for x in data)\n    n = len(xs)\n    if n == 0:\n        return None\n    if p <= 0:\n        return xs[0]\n    if p >= 1:\n        return xs[-1]\n    # rank per Excel PERCENTILE.INC\n    r = 1 + (n - 1) * p\n    k = int(r)\n    d = r - k\n    # k is 1-based index\n    if d == 0:\n        return xs[k-1]\n    # interpolate between k and k+1\n    return xs[k-1] + d * (xs[k] - xs[k-1])\n\n# ---------- Step 0/1/2/3/4: write formulas ----------\nwb = openpyxl.load_workbook(SRC)\nwsT = wb['Task']\nwsD = wb['Data']\n\nyears = [wsT.cell(row=10, column=c).value for c in year_cols]\nif any(y is None for y in years):\n    raise RuntimeError(f\"Unexpected missing year header in Task row10 H-L: {years}\")\n\nyears_const = '{' + ','.join(str(int(y)) for y in years) + '}'\n\n# Lookup blocks\nblocks = [(12,17), (19,24), (26,31)]\nfor r0, r1 in blocks:\n    for r in range(r0, r1+1):\n        for c in year_cols:\n            col_letter = get_column_letter(c)\n            # Data headers row for years are blank in this workbook, so match against constant year array\n            formula = (\n                f\"=INDEX(Data!$H$21:$L$38,\"\n                f\"MATCH($D{r},Data!$D$21:$D$38,0),\"\n                f\"MATCH({col_letter}$10,{years_const},0))\"\n            )\n            wsT.cell(row=r, column=c).value = formula\n\n# Net patient flow H35:L40\nfor i, task_row in enumerate(range(35, 41)):\n    adm = 12 + i\n    dis = 19 + i\n    bed = 26 + i\n    for c in year_cols:\n        col_letter = get_column_letter(c)\n        wsT.cell(row=task_row, column=c).value = f\"=({col_letter}{adm}-{col_letter}{dis})/{col_letter}{bed}*100\"\n\n# Summary statistics H42:L47\nstat_formulas_by_row = {\n    42: lambda col: f\"=MIN({col}35:{col}40)\",\n    43: lambda col: f\"=MAX({col}35:{col}40)\",\n    44: lambda col: f\"=MEDIAN({col}35:{col}40)\",\n    45: lambda col: f\"=AVERAGE({col}35:{col}40)\",\n    46: lambda col: f\"=PERCENTILE({col}35:{col}40,0.25)\",\n    47: lambda col: f\"=PERCENTILE({col}35:{col}40,0.75)\",\n}\nfor r in range(42, 48):\n    for c in year_cols:\n        col_letter = get_column_letter(c)\n        wsT.cell(row=r, column=c).value = stat_formulas_by_row[r](col_letter)\n\n# Weighted mean H50:L50\nfor c in year_cols:\n    col_letter = get_column_letter(c)\n    wsT.cell(row=50, column=c).value = f\"=SUMPRODUCT({col_letter}35:{col_letter}40,{col_letter}26:{col_letter}31)/SUM({col_letter}26:{col_letter}31)\"\n\nwb.save(OUT)\n\n# ---------- Step 5: compute cached numeric values ----------\n# Build lookup dict from Data\n# Data rows 21-38; series code is in column D; year values in H-L (same columns as Task years)\nlookup = {}\nfor r in range(21, 39):\n    code = wsD.cell(row=r, column=4).value\n    if code is None:\n        continue\n    lookup[code] = {}\n    for y, c in zip(years, year_cols):\n        lookup[code][int(y)] = wsD.cell(row=r, column=c).value\n\n# Compute Task values\nvalues = {}  # cell_ref -> float\n\ndef cell_ref(r, c):\n    return f\"{get_column_letter(c)}{r}\"\n\n# Fill lookup blocks\nfor r0, r1 in blocks:\n    for r in range(r0, r1+1):\n        code = wsT.cell(row=r, column=4).value\n        if code not in lookup:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"Series code {code} in Task D{r} not found in Data lookup\")\n        for y, c in zip(years, year_cols):\n            v = lookup[code][int(y)]\n            values[cell_ref(r, c)] = float(v) if v is not None else None\n\n# Net flows\nnetflow_by_col = {c: [] for c in year_cols}\nfor i, task_row in enumerate(range(35, 41)):\n    adm_row = 12 + i\n    dis_row = 19 + i\n    bed_row = 26 + i\n    for c in year_cols:\n        adm = values[cell_ref(adm_row, c)]\n        dis = values[cell_ref(dis_row, c)]\n        bed = values[cell_ref(bed_row, c)]\n        if bed in (None, 0):\n            nf = None\n        else:\n            nf = (adm - dis) / bed * 100.0\n        values[cell_ref(task_row, c)] = nf\n        netflow_by_col[c].append(nf)\n\n# Stats rows 42-47\nfor c in year_cols:\n    col_vals = [v for v in netflow_by_col[c] if v is not None]\n    if len(col_vals) != 6:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Unexpected netflow count for col {get_column_letter(c)}: {len(col_vals)}\")\n    values[cell_ref(42, c)] = min(col_vals)\n    values[cell_ref(43, c)] = max(col_vals)\n    # median\n    xs = sorted(col_vals)\n    values[cell_ref(44, c)] = (xs[2] + xs[3]) / 2.0\n    values[cell_ref(45, c)] = sum(col_vals) / len(col_vals)\n    values[cell_ref(46, c)] = excel_percentile_inc(col_vals, 0.25)\n    values[cell_ref(47, c)] = excel_percentile_inc(col_vals, 0.75)\n\n# Weighted mean row50\nfor c in year_cols:\n    net = [values[cell_ref(r, c)] for r in range(35, 41)]\n    bed = [values[cell_ref(r, c)] for r in range(26, 32)]\n    num = sum(n*w for n, w in zip(net, bed))\n    den = sum(bed)\n    values[cell_ref(50, c)] = num / den if den else None\n\n# ---------- Inject cached values into Task sheet XML ----------\n\n# Map sheet name -> sheet xml path\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(OUT, 'r') as z:\n    workbook_xml = z.read('xl/workbook.xml')\n    rels_xml = z.read('xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels')\n\nns = {\n    'main': 'http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main',\n    'rel': 'http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships'\n}\n\nwb_root = ET.fromstring(workbook_xml)\nrels_root = ET.fromstring(rels_xml)\n\nrid_to_target = {}\nfor rel in rels_root.findall('{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships}Relationship'):\n    rid_to_target[rel.attrib['Id']] = rel.attrib['Target']\n\nsheet_path = None\nfor sheet in wb_root.find('main:sheets', ns).findall('main:sheet', ns):\n    name = sheet.attrib.get('name')\n    rid = sheet.attrib.get('{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships}id')\n    if name == 'Task':\n        target = rid_to_target[rid]  # e.g. worksheets/sheet1.xml\n        sheet_path = 'xl/' + target.lstrip('/')\n        break\n\nif sheet_path is None:\n    raise RuntimeError('Could not locate Task sheet xml path')\n\n# Create modified zip\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(OUT, 'r') as zin, zipfile.ZipFile(TMP, 'w') as zout:\n    # create new row element in correct order\n                new_row = ET.Element(f\"{{{ns['main']}}}row\", {'r': str(rnum)})\n                # insert keeping ascending order\n                inserted = False\n                for idx, existing in enumerate(list(sheetdata)):\n                    er = int(existing.attrib.get('r', '0'))\n                    if er > rnum:\n                        sheetdata.insert(idx, new_row)\n                        inserted = True\n                        break\n                if not inserted:\n                    sheetdata.append(new_row)\n                row_elems[rnum] = new_row\n                return new_row\n\n            def ensure_cell(row_elem, cref):\n                # insert by column letter order\n                def col_key(ref):\n                    col = ''.join(ch for ch in ref if ch.isalpha())\n                    return col\n                inserted = False\n                existing_cells = list(row_elem.findall('main:c', ns))\n                for idx, c in enumerate(existing_cells):\n                    if col_key(c.attrib.get('r','')) > col_key(cref):\n                        row_elem.insert(idx, new_c)\n                        inserted = True\n                        break\n                if not inserted:\n                    row_elem.append(new_c)\n                return new_c\n\n            def fmt_number(x):\n                # Excel likes plain decimal; avoid scientific unless necessary\n                if x is None:\n                    return None\n                # keep enough precision but stable\n                return format(float(x), '.15g')\n\n            for cref, val in values.items():\n                vtxt = fmt_number(val)\n                if vtxt is None:\n                    continue\n                # parse row number\n                rnum = int(''.join(ch for ch in cref if ch.isdigit()))\n                row_elem = ensure_row(rnum)\n                cell_elem = ensure_cell(row_elem, cref)\n                #
